Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on 3d structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 292. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 842 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the 3d space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 292 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 842. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The 3d variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 115 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 474 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 115 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the 3d structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 474. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
